# Test list shown by the `debug_panel` console command.
#
# PERSONAL: Sol's own checklist of the changes in SJK's main branch (Sol-Vulpes/SJK).
# The file is built into the client (include_str!) and parsed once at startup. Unit
# tests in debug_panel_data.rs check it, so `cargo test` catches a broken edit.
#
# Format: one block per entry, in display order. A block starts with "[id]"; the
# lines after it are "key: value". Lines starting with '#' and blank lines are ignored.
#
#   [entry-id]   Required. Lowercase letters, digits and '-'. Ticks are saved by this
#                id, so keep it when editing text or moving the block; a new id
#                starts untested.
#   area:        Required. Console & chat, Input, Menus & settings, HUD,
#                Rendering & effects, Audio or Gameplay.
#   title:       Required. One short line.
#   change:      What changed, 1-3 lines (repeat the key for each line).
#   test:        One "To test" step per line (repeat the key); at least one.
#   note:        Optional, highlighted; repeat for more lines.
#
# Text must be ASCII (the menu font draws bytes) and each line at most 96 characters
# after the key, or it ends in an ellipsis on narrow windows.
#
# Order: the panel keeps the file's order; the "# ----" lines only mark groups.

# ---- Console & chat ----

[pr-batch-1031]
area: Gameplay
title: PRs 31/10: chat colour, GIF hiding, Change name, melee kick, duel noclip, vehicle clips
change: #315 cg_chatColor (Lumaya); #320 hide GIFs + Hidden tab (Creyon); #299 Change name (Slash).
change: #322 held melee kick restarts each kick; #323 no fakenoclip/freecam in duels (Slash).
change: #274 riders and vehicles each skip their own pilot or chassis in prediction (Lumaya).
test: Settings > Chat > All chat colour: All chat turns that colour, team and SJK chat do not.
test: SJK chat: Hide this GIF on a GIF message: reads GIF hidden; Hidden tab shows it again.
test: Profile > SJK Profile > Change name: Character tab opens with the Name field ready.
test: Hold melee kick: each kick plays from the start. In a duel, fakenoclip is refused.
test: Ride a swoop and a shuttle on a siege map: no snag or jitter against your own vehicle.

[slash-fixes-1030]
area: Menus & settings
title: Slash's fixes 30/10: config.cfg, settings search, Shaders wheel, vehicle away, Force templates
change: #288 config.cfg edited while running is loaded; deleted, it is not written back that run.
change: #290 typed values in every search result; #291 wheel past Shaders tier headings.
change: #298 no away emote on a vehicle; #309 Force templates keep Speed on saber-only servers.
test: Edit cg_fov in config.cfg while SJK runs, quit: the edit stays. Delete it, quit: not back.
test: Settings search "chat": click a later result's value and type a number: it takes it.
test: Collection > Shaders with Owned on: the wheel scrolls past every tier heading.
test: Ride a vehicle, alt-tab out a while: no away emote. JoF Fullforce FFA template: Speed kept.

[day-pc-clock]
area: Rendering & effects
title: Day and night follow the PC's clock (r_dayClock), every preset, Creyon's #296

change: Sun and sky on: the sun moves. Off (SJK, Vanilla): world 0.4 at night, a night sky.
test: SJK preset, clock on in the evening: ffa3 darkens, the sky turns to night, HUD unchanged.
test: Sun and sky on: the sun stands where the PC's hour puts it.
test: Turn it off: the world is bright again at once; the sky after a map load or vid_restart.
note: Off by default; with it off nothing changes. Check eye adaptation does not undo the night.

[remap-lighting]
area: Rendering & effects
title: Remapped surfaces keep the map's light (SJK PR #32)
change: A surface remapped to another shader keeps its own lightmap, vertex light or entity light.
change: They were drawn unlit, flat and uniformly pale, as rd-vanilla's R_RemapShader registers them.
test: On a JA+ server that remaps world textures: the surfaces show the map's light and shadow.

test: Glowing, sky and effect targets with their own stages look as before.
note: Checked offline on mp/duel6 only; not on a live server or with a $lightmap target shader.

[mover-light]
area: Rendering & effects
title: Doors, lifts and other movers block lamp light (SJK PR #39)
change: Closed doors, lifts and other moving brushes stop the light of lamps behind them.
change: Open, hidden, broken or switched-off ones let it through again.
change: The far sun shadows follow movers too, at most four times a second.
test: siege_hoth, High quality: by the closed hangar door, no lamp light leaks onto the floor.
test: Open and close that door: the light comes through as it opens and goes as it closes.
test: Break a breakable wall or switch off a force field by a lamp: no shadow stays behind.
test: Outdoors, watch a moving lift's sun shadow from far away: it follows the lift.
note: SJK_MOVER_OCCLUSION=0 leaves movers out of lamp light, to compare with the same build.
note: Not tried on screen yet: the door tracer, the partial re-bakes and the far cascade refresh.
